missing = object()
[S1013]

def json_changes(old, new, pointer=""):
    if isinstance(old, dict) and isinstance(new, dict):
        result = []
        for key in dict.fromkeys([*old, *new]):
            result.extend(json_changes(old.get(key, missing), new.get(key, missing),
                                       pointer + "/" + key.replace("~", "~0").replace("/", "~1")))
        return result
    if isinstance(old, list) and isinstance(new, list):
        result = []
        for index in range(max(len(old), len(new))):
            result.extend(json_changes(old[index] if index < len(old) else missing,
                                       new[index] if index < len(new) else missing, pointer + "/" + str(index)))
        return result
    if old is not missing and new is not missing and type(old) == type(new) and old == new:
        return []
    return [{"pointer": pointer, "old_exists": old is not missing, "old_value": None if old is missing else old,
             "new_exists": new is not missing, "new_value": None if new is missing else new}]
[S1014]

[S1019]
def sha(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
[S1020]
def git(root, *argv):
    p = subprocess.run(['git', *argv], cwd=root, capture_output=True)
    commands.append({'cwd': str(root), 'argv': ['git', *argv],
                     'exit_code': p.returncode, 'stdout_bytes': len(p.stdout),
                     'stdout_sha256': sha(p.stdout),
                     'stderr_utf8': p.stderr.decode(errors='replace')})
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(commands[-1])
    return p.stdout
[S1021]
def identity(root, commit, path):
    raw = git(root, 'show', commit + ':' + path)
    line = git(root, 'ls-tree', commit, '--', path).decode().strip()
    metadata, observed_path = line.split('\t')
    assert observed_path == path
    mode, kind, blob = metadata.split()
    return {'repo': str(root), 'commit': commit, 'path': path,
            'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'blob': blob, 'bytes': len(raw),
            'sha256': sha(raw), 'line_count': len(raw.splitlines())}, raw
[S1022]
def local_identity(path):
    raw = path.read_bytes()
    return {'path': str(path.relative_to(REPO)), 'bytes': len(raw), 'sha256': sha(raw)}

    slices, covered = [], set()
    for first, last in ranges:
        assert 1 <= first <= last <= len(lines), (key, first, last, len(lines))
        selected = b''.join(lines[first-1:last])
        covered.update(range(first,last+1))
        slices.append({'inclusive_lines': [first,last], 'bytes': len(selected),
                       'sha256': sha(selected)})
    item.update({'key': key, 'root_reading': 'DIRECT_TEXT_RANGES_READ',
                 'ranges': slices, 'entire_file_in_read_ranges': len(covered) == len(lines),
                 'outside_range_credit': 'NONE',
                 'qualification': 'Static source read only; a complete named method may be inside a partial outer method/file. No old reader FULL is inherited.'})
    source.append(item)
    source_by_key[key] = item

#!/usr/bin/env python3

[S1030]

logs = []
[S1031]
def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
[S1032]
def sha(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
[S1033]
def run(cwd, argv):
    start = utc()
    p = subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True)
    entry = {'cwd': str(cwd), 'argv': argv, 'started_utc': start,
             'finished_utc': utc(), 'exit_code': p.returncode,
             'stdout_bytes': len(p.stdout), 'stdout_sha256': sha(p.stdout),
             'stderr_bytes': len(p.stderr), 'stderr_sha256': sha(p.stderr),
             'stderr_utf8': p.stderr.decode(errors='replace')}
    if len(p.stdout) < 1024:
        entry['stdout_utf8'] = p.stdout.decode(errors='replace')
    logs.append(entry)
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(entry)
    return p.stdout
[S1034]

[S1042]

def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
[S1043]

def show(root, commit, path):
    begin = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    command = ["git", "show", commit + ":" + path]
    proc = subprocess.run(command, cwd=root, capture_output=True, check=False)
    commands.append({"argv": command, "cwd": str(root), "started_utc": begin,
                     "finished_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                     "exit_code": proc.returncode, "stdout_bytes": len(proc.stdout),
                     "stdout_sha256": sha(proc.stdout), "stderr_bytes": len(proc.stderr),
                     "stderr_sha256": sha(proc.stderr),
                     "stderr_utf8": proc.stderr.decode(errors="replace")})
    if proc.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(commands[-1])
    return proc.stdout
[S1044]

def restore(node):
    if node[0] == "v":
        return data["value_table"][node[1]]["value"]
    if node[0] == "o":
        return {key: restore(child) for key, child in node[1]}
    return [restore(child) for child in node[1]]
[S1045]

def at(value, pointer):
    if not pointer:
        return value
    if not pointer.startswith("/"):
        raise ValueError("--pointer must be empty or begin with /")
    for key in pointer[1:].split("/"):
        key = key.replace("~1", "/").replace("~0", "~")
        value = value[int(key)] if isinstance(value, list) else value[key]
    return value
[S1046]

def escape(key):
    return str(key).replace("~", "~0").replace("/", "~1")
[S1047]

def walk(value, pointer="", field=""):
    yield pointer, field, value
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key, child in value.items():
            yield from walk(child, pointer + "/" + escape(key), key)
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for index, child in enumerate(value):
            yield from walk(child, pointer + "/" + str(index), field)
[S1048]

"""Read-only, lossless navigation printer. Never assigns semantic reading credit.
[S1077]
Examples:
  python3 continuation_print.py --id GR8-COM-008 --chars 10000
  python3 continuation_print.py --id GR8-COM-008 --start 10000 --chars 10000
  python3 continuation_print.py --id GR8-COM-008 --mode leaves --chars 10000
  python3 continuation_print.py --id READSET --pointer /conditional_F05_readset
[S1078]
The default JSON view reconstructs every parsed value, object key and array order.
Leaf mode retains every leaf occurrence; repeated typed values point to the first
document/pointer. Pass --expand-repeats to print those repeated values as well.
All output is stdout; this program creates or changes no files.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
[S1079]
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
parser.add_argument("--id", help="READSET or exact contract ID; omitted lists all IDs")
parser.add_argument("--pointer", default="", help="RFC6901 pointer within this record")
parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["json", "leaves", "values"], default="json")
parser.add_argument("--expand-repeats", action="store_true")
parser.add_argument("--start", type=int, default=0, help="zero-based character offset")
parser.add_argument("--chars", type=int, default=10000, help="maximum characters; 0 means all")
args = parser.parse_args()
inventory = json.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "continuation-input-inventory.json").read_text())

values = inventory["value_table"]
[S1080]

def decode(node):
    if node[0] == "v":
        return values[node[1]]["value"]
    if node[0] == "o":
        return {key: decode(child) for key, child in node[1]}
    return [decode(child) for child in node[1]]
[S1081]

def leaf_rows(node, pointer=""):
    if node[0] == "v":
        entry = values[node[1]]
        row = {"pointer": pointer, "value_id": node[1]}
        first = {"document": entry["first_document"], "pointer": entry["first_pointer"]}
        if args.expand_repeats or first == {"document": args.id, "pointer": pointer}:
            row["exact_value"] = entry["value"]
        else:
            row["duplicate_exact_typed_value_of"] = first
        return [row]
    if not node[1]:
        return [{"pointer": pointer, "exact_empty_container": {} if node[0] == "o" else []}]
    rows = []
    children = node[1] if node[0] == "o" else enumerate(node[1])
    for key, child in children:
        rows.extend(leaf_rows(child, pointer + "/" + escape(key)))
    return rows
[S1082]

if not args.id:
    print(json.dumps([{"id": x["id"], "carrier_pointer": x["carrier_pointer"],
                       "canonical_sha256": x["parsed_value_canonical_sha256"]}
                      for x in documents], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
else:
    doc = next(x for x in documents if x["id"] == args.id)
    restored = decode(doc["tree"])
    canonical = json.dumps(restored, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
    assert hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest() == doc["parsed_value_canonical_sha256"]
    if args.mode == "json":
        value = at(restored, args.pointer)
        rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    else:
        rows = leaf_rows(doc["tree"])
        if args.pointer:
            rows = [x for x in rows if x["pointer"] == args.pointer
                    or x["pointer"].startswith(args.pointer + "/")]
        if args.mode == "values":
            unique = {}
            empty = []
            for row in rows:
                if "value_id" not in row:
                    empty.append(row)
                    continue
                number = row["value_id"]
                if number not in unique:
                    entry = values[number]
                    unique[number] = {"value_id": number, "exact_value": entry["value"],
                                      "global_first": {"document": entry["first_document"],
                                                       "pointer": entry["first_pointer"]},
                                      "all_occurrence_pointers_in_selected_document": []}
                unique[number]["all_occurrence_pointers_in_selected_document"].append(row["pointer"])
            rows = [*unique.values(), *empty]
        rendered = "\n".join(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) for x in rows)
    end = len(rendered) if args.chars == 0 else min(len(rendered), args.start + args.chars)
    if not (0 <= args.start <= len(rendered)):
        raise ValueError("--start is outside the rendered record")
    print(json.dumps({"document": args.id, "mode": args.mode, "pointer": args.pointer,
                      "start": args.start, "end_exclusive": end, "total_chars": len(rendered),
                      "next_start": end if end < len(rendered) else None,
                      "render_sha256": hashlib.sha256(rendered.encode()).hexdigest(),
                      "credit": "navigation only; output does not certify reading"}, ensure_ascii=False))
    print(rendered[args.start:end])

[S1086]

def digest(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
[S1087]

def utc():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
[S1088]

def git(*args):
    started = utc()
    argv = ["git", *args]
    proc = subprocess.run(argv, cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, check=False)
    record = {
        "argv": argv, "cwd": str(REPO), "started_utc": started,
        "finished_utc": utc(), "exit_code": proc.returncode,
        "stdout_bytes": len(proc.stdout), "stdout_sha256": digest(proc.stdout),
        "stderr_bytes": len(proc.stderr), "stderr_sha256": digest(proc.stderr),
        "stderr_utf8": proc.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
        "credit": "MECHANICAL_ONLY; not semantic full reading or behavior execution",
    }
    if len(proc.stdout) <= 1024:
        record["stdout_utf8"] = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    commands.append(record)
    if proc.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(record)
    return proc.stdout
[S1089]

def content(commit, path):
    key = (commit, path)
    if key not in data_cache:
        data_cache[key] = git("show", commit + ":" + path)
    return data_cache[key]
[S1090]

def document(commit, path):
    return json.loads(content(commit, path))
[S1091]

def identity(commit, path):
    key = (commit, path)
    if key not in identity_cache:
        raw = content(commit, path)
        mode, kind, blob = git("ls-tree", commit, "--", path).decode().split("\t")[0].split()
        identity_cache[key] = {
            "commit": commit, "path": path, "mode": mode, "kind": kind,
            "blob": blob, "bytes": len(raw), "sha256": digest(raw),
        }
    return identity_cache[key]
[S1092]

def compare_identity(expected, commit=None, path=None):
    got = identity(commit or expected["commit"], path or expected["path"])
    fields = ["mode", "blob", "bytes", "sha256"]
    differences = {k: {"expected": expected[k], "observed": got[k]}
                   for k in fields if expected[k] != got[k]}
    return {"expected": expected, "observed": got, "matched": not differences,
            "differences": differences}
[S1093]

def tree(commit, path):
    result = {}
    for line in git("ls-tree", "-r", commit, "--", path).decode().splitlines():
        header, name = line.split("\t", 1)
        result[name] = header.split()
    return result
[S1094]

def check(name, matched, evidence):
    checks.append({"name": name, "matched": bool(matched), "evidence": evidence})
[S1095]

def catalog_rows(raw):
    return Counter(line for line in raw.decode().splitlines() if line.startswith("|"))

catalogs = []
new_ids = []
for name in ["combat-requirements-wp39-40-41-42-45.md", "combat-requirements-wp49-51-52.md",
             "pokemon-rules-wp19-21-22-23-34.md"]:
    path = FORMAL + "test-catalog/" + name
    old = catalog_rows(content(DELIVERY, path))
    new = catalog_rows(content(ACTUAL, path))
    removed = list((old - new).elements())
    exceptional = []
    if "wp49-51-52" in name:
        exceptional = [row for row in old if row.startswith("| T02 |")]
        same_id = [row for row in new if row.startswith("| T02 |")]
        exception_ok = len(exceptional) == len(same_id) == 1 and old[exceptional[0]] == new[same_id[0]] == 1
        old_without_exception = old.copy()
        for row in exceptional:
            del old_without_exception[row]
        preserved = not (old_without_exception - new)
    else:
        same_id = []
        exception_ok = True
        old_without_exception = old
        preserved = not (old - new)
    added_rows = list((new - old).elements())
    ids = []
    for row in added_rows:
        value = row.split("|", 2)[1].strip()
        if value.startswith("R8-U06-"):
            ids.append(value)
    new_ids.extend(ids)
    entry = {
        "path": path, "old_table_rows": sum(old.values()), "actual_table_rows": sum(new.values()),
        "old_rows_required_unchanged": sum(old_without_exception.values()),
        "missing_required_old_row_multiset": list((old_without_exception - new).elements()),
        "removed_full_rows": removed, "AE_family_T02_original": exceptional,
        "AE_family_T02_actual_same_ID": same_id, "same_ID_exception_matched": exception_ok,
        "added_rows": added_rows, "new_R8_U06_IDs": ids,
        "matched": preserved and exception_ok,
    }
    catalogs.append(entry)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Static metadata checks only; grants no semantic or execution credit."""
import collections
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import subprocess
[S1126]
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[5]
OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RR = 'review/global-independent-review/eighth-2026-10-16-fd1df57-c84e'
R8 = 'review/remediation-eighth-20261016-prepare'
ACTUAL = '0eee6d141f33758eda34bd8ebad94a3e2b617f5a'
BINDING = '5ff355c53b73171a0277c95af97e1cbd40eb8b28'
[S1127]
def git(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(['git', *args], cwd=ROOT)
[S1128]
def load(path, commit=ACTUAL):
    return json.loads(git('show', f'{commit}:{path}'))
[S1129]
manifest = load(f'{R8}/integration/U06/actual-review-input.json', BINDING)
batch = manifest['batches'][0]
expected = batch['required_ids'] + batch['selected_optional_ids']
contracts = load(f'{RR}/handoff/contracts.zh.json')
readset = load(f'{RR}/handoff/batches/R8-U06/readset.json')
dispositions = load(f'{R8}/candidates/R8-U06/evidence/dispositions.json')
candidate_result = load(batch['candidate_review_result_path'])
changes = load(f'{R8}/integration/U06/actual-changes.json')
[S1130]
alignment = []
for item in readset['contracts']:
    value = contracts
    for part in item['pointer'].strip('/').split('/'):
        value = value[int(part)] if isinstance(value, list) else value[part]
    alignment.append({'id': item['id'], 'pointer': item['pointer'],
                      'resolved_id': value.get('id'),
                      'identity_matches': value.get('id') == item['id'],
                      'semantic_read': False})
[S1131]
target_links = []
anchors = []
for path in batch['actual_target_paths']:
    text = git('show', f'{ACTUAL}:{path}').decode()
    ids = re.findall(r'<a\s+id="([^"]+)"\s*>', text)
    counts = collections.Counter(ids)
    anchors.append({'path': path, 'explicit_anchor_counts': dict(counts),
                    'duplicate_explicit_anchors': [key for key, n in counts.items() if n > 1]})
    for match in re.finditer(r'\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)]+)\)', text):
        href = match.group(1)
        if re.match(r'[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*:', href) or href.startswith('#'):
            continue
        clean = href.split('#', 1)[0].split('?', 1)[0]
        relative = PurePosixPath(path).parent / clean
        normalized = []
        for part in relative.parts:
            if part == '..':
                normalized.pop()
            elif part != '.':
                normalized.append(part)
        dest = '/'.join(normalized)
        result = subprocess.run(['git', 'cat-file', '-e', f'{ACTUAL}:{dest}'], cwd=ROOT,
                                capture_output=True)
        target_links.append({'source': path, 'line': text[:match.start()].count('\n') + 1,
                             'href': href, 'resolved_path': dest, 'exists': result.returncode == 0,
                             'qualification': 'FILE_EXISTENCE_ONLY; section meaning not reviewed'})
[S1132]
datasets = {'manifest': expected,
            'readset_contracts': [x['id'] for x in readset['contracts']],
            'author_dispositions': [x['id'] for x in dispositions['records']],
            'candidate_results': [x['id'] for x in candidate_result['records']],
            'actual_changes': [x['id'] for x in changes['records']]}
result = {
    'schema': 'r8-u06-actual-structural-checks/v1',
    'qualification': 'TOOL_VALIDATION_ONLY_NO_SEMANTIC_PASS',
    'artifact_commit': ACTUAL, 'input_binding_commit': BINDING,
    'record_sets': datasets,
    'all_record_sets_exact_14_ids': all(set(v) == set(expected) and len(v) == 14 for v in datasets.values()),
    'contract_pointer_alignment': alignment,
    'relative_markdown_links': target_links,
    'all_relative_markdown_file_targets_exist': all(x['exists'] for x in target_links),
    'explicit_anchor_inventory': anchors,
    'duplicate_explicit_anchor_total': sum(len(x['duplicate_explicit_anchors']) for x in anchors),
    'candidate_report_declared_pass': candidate_result['summary']['candidate_PASS'],
    'candidate_report_bound_candidate': candidate_result['candidate_commit'],
    'candidate_review_eligibility_confirmed': False,
    'candidate_or_author_full_read_credit_transferred': False,
    'actual_integrated_PASS': False,
    'business_execution': 'NOT_EXECUTED',
}
(OUT / 'structural-checks.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n')
print(json.dumps({k: result[k] for k in ['all_record_sets_exact_14_ids',
      'all_relative_markdown_file_targets_exist', 'duplicate_explicit_anchor_total']}, ensure_ascii=False))
